Abstract: Embodied video world models predict scene changes caused by robot actions, yet generated object responses may remain inconsistent with task intent and contact dynamics. Critical responses in embodied scenes often concentrate in local regions and specific time intervals. Inspired by chain-of-thought reasoning, we introduce ReactWorld, an embodied video generation model guided by structured interaction anticipation. In the generation backbone's feature space, an interaction response field predictor anticipates how the interaction will unfold, explicitly describing participant roles, future motion response intensity, and local motion changes. A response feedback adapter forms increments to object features from the difference between the object branch's outputs with predicted and zeroed local dynamics inputs. It also converts predicted displacements into positional biases for temporal attention. These two mechanisms guide object feature updates and information retrieval across frames, respectively. Through interaction anticipation and generative feedback, ReactWorld achieves an average score of 77.51 on RBench, outperforming PhysisForcing by 3.29 points with improvements in all nine categories. On EZS-Bench, it achieves a Domain score of 86.98 and an average score of 81.87.

</details>


### [69] [RDT-3D: Resolution-Decoupled Training for High-Fidelity 3D Geometry Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06141)
*Jiale Zhang,Yang Liu,Chengyang Li,Jin Yuan,Qianxi Jia,Taicheng Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文揭示粗到细3D生成中训练-推理分辨率不一致问题，提出分辨解耦训练(RDT)与Dual3D-300K大规模配对数据集，提升高分辨率几何细节生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有粗到细框架用RoPE对局部查询进行条件化时，训练阶段坐标引导与几何监督的分辨率一致，而推理阶段却用粗坐标解码高分辨率几何，产生严重分布偏移，导致细节退化。

Method: 提出Resolution-Decoupled Training (RDT)：将RoPE引导锚定在512分辨率，同时在1536分辨率上进行几何监督，实现稳健的细节外推；并构建Dual3D-300K，包含30万个水密3D点云、在512与1536分辨率间显式对齐的大规模配对数据集。

Result: 大量实验表明RDT显著改善尖锐几何区域的生成质量，Dual3D-300K为高分辨率3D生成提供可扩展基准。

Conclusion: 通过解耦训练与推理的分辨率并以大规模对齐数据支撑，可有效缓解分布偏移，提升高保真3D几何生成的细节表现。

Abstract: High-fidelity 3D geometry generation requires maintaining global structural coherence alongside fine-grained surface details. While recent coarse-to-fine frameworks leverage Rotary Position Embeddings (RoPE) to condition local queries, we reveal a critical training-inference resolution discrepancy: coordinate guidance and geometric supervision are aligned during training, whereas inference decodes high-resolution geometry from coarse spatial coordinates. This mismatch introduces a severe distribution shift that degrades fine details. To overcome this, we propose Resolution-Decoupled Training (RDT), which anchors RoPE guidance at 512 resolution while supervising geometry at 1536, enabling robust detail extrapolation. Additionally, to eliminate high-resolution data bottlenecks, we present Dual3D-300K, a large-scale paired dataset comprising 300K watertight 3D point clouds explicitly aligned across 512 and 1536 resolutions. Extensive experiments demonstrate that RDT substantially improves sharp geometric regions, while Dual3D-300K offers a scalable benchmark for high-resolution 3D generation.

</details>


### [70] [PTQ4SAM3: Semantics-Preserving Post-Training Quantization for Segment Anything Model 3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06143)
*Xi Xiao,Yunbei Zhang,Lin Zhao,Janet Wang,Xinru Jiang,Yanshu Li,Chenrui Ma,Pan Wang,Tianyang Wang,Xiao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SAM 3 可依据文本提示分割并跨帧跟踪，但显存与计算开销大；训练后量化（PTQ）虽降成本，却会导致“提示对齐漂移”。作者提出语义保持量化（SPQ），以概念敏感参数加权校准、保留残差特征、校正视频记忆均值误差，在离线、无推理额外算子的前提下显著提升量化后性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAM 3 的记忆与计算成本使部署困难，而现成 PTQ 会引入“提示对齐漂移”：量化模型可能选中错误目标或在跨帧跟踪中丢失目标。受控实验表明，扰动低方差特征方向会强烈影响目标选择，说明量化对语义敏感方向特别脆弱。

Method: 提出 Semantics-Preserving Quantization（SPQ），含三部分：(1) 给概念敏感参数更高校准权重；(2) 保留残差特征；(3) 校正视频记忆中的均值误差。以固定文本概念指导校准，无需任务标注；三部分均离线运行，不增加推理算子。

Result: W4A8 图像编码器下，RefCOCO Pr@0.5 由 PTQ4SAM 的 42.1 提升至 72.5，同时保持 54.8 COCO mask AP；在 LVIS、RefCOCO 变体与 SA-V 上均有一致增益；CLIP 研究亦显示图文检索改善。

Conclusion: SPQ 通过保护概念敏感方向与校正视频记忆漂移，有效缓解量化引起的提示对齐漂移，在不增加推理开销的情况下兼顾压缩效率与分割/跟踪性能，并具备向图文检索任务迁移的潜力。

Abstract: Segment Anything Model 3 (SAM 3) can segment objects from text prompts and track them across video, but its memory and computation costs make deployment difficult. Post-training quantization (PTQ) reduces these costs by using fewer bits. However, a quantized model can select the wrong object or lose track of its target across frames. We call this prompt-alignment drift. Controlled tests show that perturbing low-variance feature directions strongly affects target selection. We propose Semantics-Preserving Quantization (SPQ), which gives more calibration weight to concept-sensitive parameters, preserves residual features, and corrects the mean error in video memory. Fixed text concepts guide calibration without task annotations. All three components operate offline and add no inference operators. With a W4A8 image encoder, SPQ improves RefCOCO Pr@0.5 from 42.1 to 72.5 over PTQ4SAM while retaining 54.8 COCO mask AP. Experiments on LVIS, RefCOCO variants, and SA-V show consistent gains. A CLIP study also shows improved image-text retrieval.

</details>


### [71] [Grounding in the Dark: Text-Only Training for Zero-Shot Video Temporal Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06149)
*Divyanshu Mishra,Sabine Sternig,Rakshith Shetty,Erhan Gundogdu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出在训练时完全不用成对视频数据、仅用文本视频描述训练LLM完成时序定位（推理时才接收视频）的设定；构建134K样本语料，基于Qwen3-8B+LoRA与CLIP/LLM2CLIP文本嵌入，并设计免训练的Nearest Entity Replacement（NER）桥接模态鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频时序任务通常依赖昂贵的成对视频-文本数据与精确时序标注；作者希望验证纯文本LLM能否仅凭文本化视频描述学会时序定位，从而摆脱训练阶段的成对多模态标注依赖。

Method: 生成含至多100个时间步、规模134K的文本序列语料；在CLIP或LLM2CLIP文本嵌入上以LoRA微调Qwen3-8B；在未使用两基准视频训练的Charades-STA与QV-Highlights上评测；提出免训练映射NER，用每帧最近文本实体嵌入的归一化均值表示该帧，使推理输入分布贴近训练分布。

Result: LLM2CLIP因文本编码器支持更长输入，对细粒度描述表征更优（Temporal-TextVid上61.54 vs 53.86 mtIoU）且迁移更好；但推理时直接以帧嵌入替换文本仅得Charades-STA 4.99、QV-Highlights 11.12 mtIoU，暴露显著模态鸿沟。NER提升至14.45与20.72 mtIoU，分别超过四类模态鸿沟基线中最优者5.00与1.71个点。

Conclusion: 仅文本训练可实现从文本到视频的部分迁移，NER可有效缓解模态鸿沟；更可靠的时序定位仍需文本与视觉表征更好对齐的编码器。

Abstract: Video-LLMs are typically trained on paired video-text data for temporal tasks, yet collecting precise temporal annotations and training on large-scale video datasets are both costly. We investigate whether a text-only LLM, with no vision-language pretraining, can be trained on textual video descriptions alone to perform temporal grounding and receive video only at inference time. This setting removes paired video data from training altogether and is a step toward training multimodal models without paired annotations. We generate a 134K-example corpus of textual sequences with up to 100 time steps, adapt Qwen3-8B with LoRA on CLIP or LLM2CLIP text embeddings, and evaluate on Charades-STA and QV-Highlights without training on videos from either benchmark. LLM2CLIP, whose text encoder supports longer inputs, represents detailed descriptions better than CLIP (61.54 versus 53.86 mtIoU on Temporal-TextVid) and transfers them better to video. Still, replacing text with frame embeddings at inference yields only 4.99 mtIoU on Charades-STA and 11.12 on QV-Highlights, revealing a large modality gap. To bridge it, we propose Nearest Entity Replacement (NER), a training-free mapping that represents each video frame by the normalized mean of its nearest text-entity embeddings, so that the model receives the kind of input it was trained on. NER reaches 14.45 mtIoU on Charades-STA and 20.72 on QV-Highlights, outperforming the strongest of four families of modality-gap baselines by 5.00 and 1.71 points. NER thus enables partial transfer from text to video, while more reliable grounding will likely require encoders with better-aligned text and visual representations.

</details>


### [72] [DSRS: Teaching MLLMs to Perceive, Reason, and Segment in Complex Driving Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06160)
*Sicen Guo,Zhengfei Song,Kuanhsun Chen,Xu Huang,Ping Zhong,Wei Ye,Shaoyi Du,Rui Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出驾驶场景推理分割任务DSRS并构建基准数据集，设计DSRS-Net通过融合几何特征缓解MLLM仅RGB预训练导致的模态表征失配，在DSRS及多个语言驱动分割任务上取得更优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理分割框架依赖具备广泛世界知识的MLLM以类人方式提取语义并生成掩码，但缺乏场景特定（scene-specific）感知能力，难以应对驾驶场景中的隐式指令、空间关系推断、潜在风险检测、困难条件与交通规则理解。

Method: 定义驾驶场景推理分割（DSRS）任务并建立对应基准数据集；提出DSRS-Net架构，在不破坏原有跨模态融合机制的前提下无缝注入几何特征，从而缓解MLLM因仅RGB预训练造成的模态表征失配，并增强几何、上下文与语言特征的融合。

Result: 大量实验表明DSRS-Net在DSRS任务及多个公开数据集上的其他语言驱动分割任务中均取得优越性能，验证了方法的有效性与泛化性。

Conclusion: 将场景特定几何信息引入MLLM推理分割流程可有效弥补通用世界知识与驾驶场景需求之间的差距，DSRS任务及DSRS-Net为自动驾驶场景理解提供了新的研究方向与基线。

Abstract: Existing reasoning segmentation frameworks typically employ multimodal large language models (MLLMs) with broad world knowledge to extract semantics in a human-like manner and generate corresponding segmentation masks. Nevertheless, these frameworks lack the necessary scene-specific awareness. This paper introduces driving scene reasoning segmentation (DSRS), a scene-specific task that requires models to interpret implicit instructions, infer spatial relations, detect potential risks, handle challenging conditions, and comprehend underlying traffic rules, ultimately generating accurate segmentation masks. Meanwhile, we establish a benchmark dataset to facilitate future research on the DSRS task. To pioneer this task, we propose DSRS-Net, a novel architecture with advanced reasoning segmentation capability for automated driving. Specifically, DSRS-Net seamlessly infuses geometric features for deeper reasoning segmentation, without compromising the original multimodal fusion mechanism. This strategy effectively mitigates the modality representation mismatch in MLLMs caused by RGB-only pretraining, while enhancing the integration of geometric, contextual, and linguistic features. Extensive experiments demonstrate the superior performance of DSRS-Net in both driving scene reasoning segmentation and other language-driven segmentation tasks across various public datasets. The source code and dataset will be released upon publication.

</details>


### [73] [Reasoning-Aware Cross-Modal Association Unlearning in Multimodal Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06168)
*Xinghao Li,Chenchen Tan,Youyang Qu,Shujie Cui,Xingliang Yuan,Cunjian Chen,Chi Liu,Longxiang Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Visual-Keyed Counterfactual Rebinding（VKCR），一种参数高效的多模态"跨模态关联遗忘"框架，在指定视觉语境下抑制特定"图像→知识"关联行为，同时保留该知识在允许语境中的可访问性，并给出CLEAR-R与MLLMU-R两个评测基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大推理模型（MLRMs）在推理中会通过跨模态关联访问敏感信息，若某视觉输入不应再提供该信息的访问权，则存在隐私风险。现有多模态遗忘方法多针对样本、概念或知识内容本身，未将知识与"访问它的跨模态关联"分离；且在推理模型中，目标信息可能从最终答案中消失却仍残留在中间推理链里，或被错误的替代知识填充。

Method: 1）将编辑定位到一个紧凑的、以关联为条件的参数子空间（association-conditioned parameter subspace），实现参数高效编辑；2）在任务输出层面降低对指定目标的支撑度；3）反事实重绑定：对承载目标或无支撑的续写予以惩罚，同时偏好有效的非敏感替代续写，从而改写受影响的推理行为。

Result: 实验表明VKCR显著降低了图像条件下的目标泄漏与幻觉性重关联，且在异构任务表述下同样有效，同时保持非敏感视觉推理能力与允许的纯文本知识访问。

Conclusion: 将多模态遗忘的问题定义从"知识内容"细化为"跨模态关联"，并以局部化参数编辑+反事实重绑定实现关联级遗忘，配合CLEAR-R/MLLMU-R对泄漏、幻觉重关联、推理保持与模态特异性效用进行系统评估，为推理模型中的隐私保护型关联编辑提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Multimodal large reasoning models (MLRMs) integrate visual inputs with textual and parametric knowledge, allowing sensitive information to be accessed through cross-modal associations during reasoning. This raises privacy concerns when a visual input should no longer provide access to such information. Machine unlearning can remove this influence without retraining the model from scratch. However, multimodal unlearning methods mainly target samples, concepts, or knowledge content, without separating the knowledge from the cross-modal association through which it is accessed. In reasoning models, target information may disappear from the final answer while remaining in intermediate reasoning, or be replaced by incorrect substitute knowledge. We therefore study cross-modal association unlearning, where the forget target is a designated visual-to-knowledge association: the model should suppress target-bearing behavior under the designated visual context while preserving access to the same knowledge in permitted contexts. We propose Visual-Keyed Counterfactual Rebinding (VKCR), a parameter-efficient framework for cross-modal association editing. VKCR first localizes the edit to a compact association-conditioned parameter subspace. It then reduces support for the designated target at task outputs and counterfactually rebinds the affected reasoning behavior by discouraging target-bearing or unsupported continuations while favoring valid non-sensitive alternatives. We further introduce CLEAR-R and MLLMU-R to evaluate target leakage, hallucinated reassociation, reasoning preservation, and modality-specific utility. Experiments show that VKCR substantially reduces image-conditioned target leakage and hallucinated reassociation, including across heterogeneous task formulations, while preserving non-sensitive visual reasoning and permitted text-only knowledge.

</details>


### [74] [Rohbau3D-Semantics: Dense Semantic and Instance Labels for Point Clouds of Indoor Shell Construction Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06180)
*Lukas Rauch,Thomas Braml*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Rohbau3D-Semantics 数据集，为既有 Rohbau3D 壳体施工现场激光扫描数据补充稠密语义与实例级标注，包含 14 个测站、504 个点云场景、17 类建筑构件加 1 个背景类，并提供全景/立方图多模态表示生成脚本与语义分割基准实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有资源存在不足：合成室内数据集缺乏真实扫描特性，网格重建导出的基准失真，建筑领域专用数据规模小。壳体施工阶段场景复杂且标注稀缺，缺少兼具真实激光扫描特性与高密度人工标注的基准。

Method: 基于地面激光扫描仪在 14 个测站采集 504 个点云场景，采用扫描仪中心局部坐标系保存；逐点人工标注并经专家监督与全场景复核，标注 17 类建筑构件与 1 类背景，另附实例标注；同时提供从 3D 数组导出彩色、LiDAR 反射强度、深度、表面法向、语义与实例标签的几何对齐全景图与立方图脚本，并给出基准划分与语义分割基线实验。

Result: 构建了保留真实扫描特性（非均匀采样、测量伪影、反射、精细几何细节）的高质量点云语义/实例基准；提供多模态全景表示与标准化数据划分，并报告语义分割基线结果，形成可复现的评测基准。

Conclusion: Rohbau3D-Semantics 以精确地面激光扫描、稠密标注和多样化壳体施工场景，弥合了合成室内数据集、网格派生基准与小型建筑专用资源之间的差距，可支撑 3D 场景理解、施工现场分析与多模态场景分析研究，数据集已公开。

Abstract: We present the Rohbau3D-Semantics dataset, which adds dense semantic and instance-level labels to laser scans of the previously released Rohbau3D domain data from shell construction sites. The dataset comprises 504 point cloud scenes from 14 survey sites, each captured with a terrestrial laser scanner and stored in a scanner-centered local coordinate system. Unlike point clouds sampled from reconstructed meshes or synthetic building models, the data preserve the characteristic properties of real laser scanning, including non-uniform sampling, measurement artifacts, reflections, and fine geometric detail. Combined with manual labels, expert oversight during pointwise annotation, and full-scene review, this yields a high-quality benchmark for semantic scene understanding in complex built environments. The dataset includes 17 class labels of building components and one background class, along with instance annotations for object-level analysis. In addition to the 3D point clouds, the release also provides scripts for deriving geometrically aligned panoramic and cube-map representations of color, LiDAR reflection intensity, depth, surface normals, semantic labels, and instance labels from the 3D arrays. We describe the acquisition pipeline, annotation protocol, quality-control process, and panorama-generation procedure, and provide benchmark splits and baseline experiments for semantic segmentation. By combining precise terrestrial laser scans, dense annotations, and a wide variety of shell-construction scenes, Rohbau3D-Semantics bridges the gap among synthetic indoor datasets, mesh-derived benchmarks, and smaller construction-specific resources. The dataset is intended to support research on 3D scene understanding, construction-site analytics, and multimodal scene analysis in the built environment.
  Access and download the full dataset via https://github.com/RauchLukas/rohbau3d

</details>


### [75] [Annotator-Free Object Detection via Foundation Model Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06195)
*Yasiru Ranasinghe,Vishal M. Patel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种无需目标数据集人工标注的目标检测框架，融合分割与视觉-语言基础模型并配合自精炼训练循环，在零样本设置下于COCO/LVIS达到60.8/47.4 AP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有目标检测受限于高昂的标注成本与监督模型的闭集假设，难以扩展到新类别与新域；同时LVLM查询成本高昂。作者希望在不使用目标数据集任何人工标注的前提下实现对新类别、新域的检测适配。

Method: 模块化流水线：利用预训练分割模型与视觉-语言模型自主生成分割候选，通过多模态推理（LVLM）赋予语义标签；引入基于地标的聚类机制，将标注在图像簇间摊销，提升标签一致性并降低LVLM查询开销；再用轻量级开放集检测器构成自精炼训练循环，迭代改进定位与分类。方法保持与现有检测架构兼容。

Result: 在COCO、LVIS、ODinW-35与Roboflow-100上全面评估，零样本设置下COCO 60.8 AP、LVIS 47.4 AP，显著优于当前先进的开放词表与长尾检测方法。

Conclusion: 该框架无需目标数据集标注即可适配完全未见类别与域，性能优于现有方法，并为标注不可行的真实未知环境检测系统提供可扩展基础。

Abstract: Object detection research remains constrained by high annotation costs and the closed-set assumptions of supervised models, limiting scalability to novel categories. We present a target-dataset annotation-free object detection framework that eliminates the need for human labeling on the target dataset by leveraging the complementary strengths of pretrained foundation models, including segmentation and vision-language systems, combined with a self-refining training loop. To further reduce reliance on expensive LVLM queries, we introduce a landmark-based clustering mechanism that amortizes labeling across image clusters, improving label consistency while lowering computational cost. Our method autonomously generates segmentation proposals, assigns semantic labels through multimodal reasoning, and iteratively improves both localization and classification using a lightweight open-set detector. This modular pipeline enables adaptation to completely unseen object classes and domains without any human-provided target-dataset annotations, while maintaining compatibility with existing detection architectures. Evaluations on COCO, LVIS, ODinW-35, and Roboflow-100 demonstrate substantial performance gains over state-of-the-art open-vocabulary and long-tail detection methods, achieving 60.8 AP on COCO and 47.4 AP on LVIS under zero-shot settings. Beyond strong benchmark results, our approach provides a scalable foundation for real-world detection systems in unknown environments, where annotation is infeasible.

</details>


### [76] [BiCG-Net: Target-Specific Image Quality Assessment via Observation Modeling and In-Image Differencing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06203)
*Yibu Yang,Tingqiong Cui,Liming Lei,Hao Xiong,Jun Xu,Siyuan Liu,Guanghui Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出面向图像中特定目标区域（如车体）的质量评估，而非全图NR-IQA；通过嵌套采样构建由目标框向外扩张的patch序列并从序列的相对结构推断质量，提出BiCG-Net学生网络蒸馏11教师集成，在CSIQ/LIVEC上PLCC达0.9009/0.9133，超过最佳单教师与教师共识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全图NR-IQA在目标仅占画面小比例时会被背景主导，且缺少目标级人类MOS的公开基准，因此需要专门估计目标区域质量。

Method: 采用嵌套采样：从目标框向外扩张的一系列patch序列，基于序列相对结构推断质量。提出BiCG-Net，通过线性观测模型与图像内差分机制蒸馏11教师集成，理论上可抵消目标项与教师特有偏置；学生网络在各数据集上独立训练与测试，并进行跨基准零样本迁移评估。

Result: CSIQ与LIVEC上对学生与人类MOS的PLCC分别为0.9009和0.9133，超过最佳单教师与教师共识；自建无MOS铁路数据集上对弱标签PLCC为0.9855。CSIQ→LIVEC迁移下冻结学生PLCC为0.8416，超共识0.1947。残差分析显示预测残差与背景污染、教师离散度、标签极端性显著相关。

Conclusion: 学生网络并非模仿教师，而是学习从教师读数相对结构到真值标签的新映射，因此能提取共识之外的信息；BiCG-Net定位为离线目标级质量审计与标签生成引擎，其39,482参数学生网络作为紧凑的统一层，将异构教师读数映射到共同质量尺度。

Abstract: We estimate the quality of a specific target region (e.g., a train body) in an image, rather than the whole image. This differs fundamentally from whole-image No-Reference Image Quality Assessment (NR-IQA): when the target occupies a small fraction of the frame, the whole-image score is dominated by background, and no public benchmark provides target-level human Mean Opinion Score (MOS). We estimate target quality via nested sampling - a sequence of patches expanding outward from the target box - and infer quality from the relative structure of this sequence.
  This motivates BiCG-Net, a student network that distills an 11-teacher ensemble through a linear observation model and an in-image differencing mechanism, which provably cancels the target term and teacher-specific bias. We train and test the student independently on each dataset, and evaluate zero-shot transfer across benchmarks. On CSIQ and LIVEC, the student reaches PLCCs of 0.9009 and 0.9133 against human MOS, exceeding the best single teacher and the teacher consensus. On a self-built MOS-free railway dataset, the student reaches a PLCC of 0.9855 against a weak label.
  The student exceeds its teachers because it learns a new mapping from the relative structure of teacher readings to the ground-truth label, rather than mimicking them. Residual analysis on CSIQ and LIVEC shows the prediction residual is significantly correlated with background contamination, teacher dispersion, and label extremity, demonstrating the student extracts information the consensus does not contain. Under CSIQ-to-LIVEC transfer, the frozen student reaches a PLCC of 0.8416, exceeding the consensus by 0.1947. We position BiCG-Net as an offline target-level quality auditing and label-generation engine; its 39,482-parameter student is compact by design, acting as a unifying layer mapping heterogeneous teacher readings onto a common quality scale.

</details>


### [77] [FedXCrop: Privacy-Preserving Crop Disease Detection via Federated Learning with Post-Aggregation Explainability under Non-IID Farm Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06221)
*Kriti Saini,Manikandan P*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FedXCrop联邦学习作物病害分类流程，并定量评估全局模型解释质量；MobileNetV2集中训练达99.38%，联邦FedAvg在IID下98.81%，标签偏斜下降0.6–1.6个百分点；Grad-CAM较稳健，SmoothGrad受图像结构影响大，FedProx无显著收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集中式训练需农场共享原始叶片图像，存在隐私与数据共享受限问题；同时缺乏解释的预测对施药决策支持有限，因此需在联邦框架下兼顾隐私保护与解释可信度的定量评估。

Method: 基于MobileNetV2，在PlantVillage（38类、5,428张固定测试集）上做集中训练；联邦侧以ImageNet预训练权重初始化，5个客户端训练30轮，对比FedAvg与FedProx在IID及Dirichlet标签偏斜（α=1、0.5、0.1）下的表现；在380张测试图上用Grad-CAM与SmoothGrad评估解释能量集中于叶片的程度、联邦与集中模型的Spearman相关性、病灶定位及模型随机化检验。

Result: 集中训练测试准确率99.38%（3个随机种子标准差0.05）；FedAvg在IID、α=1、0.5、0.1下分别为98.81%、98.81%、98.59%、97.78%；FedProx各异质度下与FedAvg无显著差异（McNemar p>0.3）。解释评估中两方法对叶片的能量归因高于随机（0.70–0.78 vs 0.60），联邦与集中模型Grad-CAM的Spearman相关为0.74–0.76；异质度升高时一致性下降，病灶定位仅略高于随机，随机化检验显示SmoothGrad图主要依赖图像结构而非学习参数。

Conclusion: 在此设定下，标签偏斜使联邦精度较集中训练下降0.6–1.6个百分点；FedAvg基本保持Grad-CAM行为一致，FedProx无可测收益；联邦模型的解释质量应被定量纳入评估，且SmoothGrad类解释的可靠性需谨慎解读。

Abstract: Deep learning can classify crop diseases accurately from benchmark leaf images, but centralized training requires farms to share raw data, and predictions without explanations offer limited support for treatment decisions. We present FedXCrop, a federated crop disease classification pipeline that quantitatively evaluates explanations of the aggregated global model. A MobileNetV2 model trained centrally on PlantVillage achieved 99.38% test accuracy (standard deviation 0.05 over three seeds) on a fixed test set of 5,428 images across 38 classes. Federated models initialized with ImageNet weights were trained for 30 rounds across five clients. FedAvg achieved 98.81% accuracy with identically distributed clients and 98.81%, 98.59%, and 97.78% under Dirichlet label skew (alpha = 1, 0.5, and 0.1, respectively). FedProx was not significantly different from FedAvg at any heterogeneity level (McNemar p > 0.3). Grad-CAM and SmoothGrad explanations were evaluated on 380 test images. Both attributed more energy to the leaf than chance (0.70-0.78 versus 0.60), while Grad-CAM maps from federated and centralized models showed Spearman correlations of 0.74-0.76. Agreement decreased as heterogeneity increased, lesion localization was only modestly above chance, and a model-randomization test indicated that SmoothGrad maps depended substantially on image structure rather than learned parameters. In this setting, label skew reduced federated accuracy by 0.6-1.6 percentage points relative to centralized training. FedAvg largely preserved Grad-CAM behavior, while FedProx provided no measurable benefit.

</details>


### [78] [Homology Features for X-Ray Hyperspectral Images Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06230)
*Fethi Harkat,Guillaume Gey,Valérie Perrier,Kévin Polisano*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种融合持续同调拓扑特征与卷积/图神经网络的混合架构，用于多能XRT高光谱图像分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多能X射线透射（XRT）高光谱图像存在高维冗余与非线性光谱重叠，传统分类框架难以处理；UMAP等流形学习虽能保留非线性相关性改善光谱表征，但未显式利用嵌入流形的几何结构进行分割。

Method: 在UMAP投影后的超像素点云上计算Vietoris-Rips持续同调，提取编码光谱异质性的H1环状结构；将拓扑描述子与像素级CNN特征通过广播机制融合，把超像素级图表示传播至全空间分辨率。

Result: 在XRT多能图像上的实验表明，该混合模型显著优于仅CNN基线和超像素池化融合基线。

Conclusion: 拓扑特征可为局部光谱-空间描述子提供互补的判别信息，验证了拓扑深度学习在该类高光谱分类任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Characterizing Multi-Energy X-ray Transmission (XRT) hyperspectral images presents significant challenges for traditional classification frameworks due to high-dimensional redundancy and non-linear spectral overlaps. Although manifold learning techniques such as UMAP enhance spectral representation by preserving non-linear correlations, they do not explicitly utilize the geometric structure of the embedded manifold for segmentation. This study introduces a hybrid architecture that integrates topological features derived from persistent homology with convolutional and graph neural networks. The approach computes Vietoris-Rips persistent homology on UMAP-projected superpixel point clouds to capture H 1 cyclic structures that encode spectral heterogeneity. These topological descriptors are combined with pixel-level CNN features through a broadcasting mechanism that propagates superpixel-level graph representations to full spatial resolution. Experimental results on XRT multi-energy imagery demonstrate that the proposed hybrid model significantly outperforms both CNN-only and superpixel-pooling fusion baselines, suggesting that topological features provide complementary discriminative information to local spectralspatial descriptors.

</details>


### [79] [CAPE-JEPA - Cross-modal Adversarial Probabilistic Embedding JEPA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06247)
*Aniket Junghare,Biplab Banerjee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CAPE-JEPA，一种概率化跨模态联合嵌入预测架构，用于遥感跨模态图像检索（RS-CMIR），通过高斯预测器、对抗分布正则与训练内对比学习，在BEN-14K与fMoW上显著超越X-JEPA及SkySense、CROMA等基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAR与多光谱等异构传感器存在巨大物理鸿沟，RS-CMIR需要兼具模态不变性与语义判别性的表征。现有JEPA类预测式自监督方法（如X-JEPA）将跨模态对齐建模为确定性隐空间预测，隐含假设各patch可预测性一致，且未解决跨模态分布对齐问题。

Method: (i) 跨注意力高斯预测器：以对侧模态上下文为条件，输出逐patch的均值与方差(μ, σ²)，用负对数似然训练以刻画跨传感器预测不确定性；(ii) 对抗式分布正则：结合单模态各向同性高斯正则与新型跨模态对抗变体，通过最坏情形Epps-Pulley特征函数检验对齐模态分布，且无需显式样本配对；(iii) 训练内对比学习：采用注意力池化头实现场景级判别性语义接地。

Result: 在BEN-14K上较X-JEPA最高提升13.74个点，在fMoW上提升15.59个点；在SAR、多光谱、RGB模态的单模态与跨模态检索方向上均优于包括SkySense与CROMA在内的所有基线，且参数量更少（407.30M vs. 432.72M）。

Conclusion: 将跨模态预测由确定性回归转为概率化不确定性建模，并辅以无配对的分布级对抗对齐与场景级对比监督，可在更低参数成本下实现更优的跨模态遥感检索表征，验证了分布对齐与判别性接地对RS-CMIR的必要性。

Abstract: Cross-modal remote sensing image retrieval (RS-CMIR) requires representations that are simultaneously modality-invariant and semantically discriminative, despite the profound physical gap between heterogeneous sensors such as Synthetic Aperture Radar (SAR) and multispectral imagery. Recent JEPA-based Predictive self-supervised approaches such as X-JEPA formulate cross-modal alignment as a deterministic latent forecasting task, implicitly assuming uniform patch predictability and leaving cross-modal distributional alignment unaddressed. We propose CAPE-JEPA, a probabilistic cross-modal Joint Embedding Predictive Architecture with three contributions: (i) a cross-attentional Gaussian predictor outputting per-patch parameters (mu, sigma^2) conditioned on opposite-modality context, trained via negative log-likelihood to model inherent cross-sensor prediction uncertainty; (ii) adversarial distributional regularization combining unimodal isotropic Gaussian regularization with a novel cross-modal adversarial variant that aligns modality distributions via worst-case Epps-Pulley characteristic function tests without explicit sample pairing; and (iii) in-training contrastive learning with attention-pooled heads for scene-level discriminative grounding. Evaluated on BigEarthNet-14K (BEN-14K) and fMoW across SAR, multispectral, and RGB modalities, CAPE-JEPA surpasses X-JEPA by up to 13.74 points on BEN-14K and 15.59 points on fMoW, outperforming all baselines including SkySense and CROMA across both unimodal and cross-modal directions while using fewer parameters (407.30M vs. 432.72M).

</details>


### [80] [CrossPressor: Scalable Cross-View Fusion for Feed-Forward 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06258)
*Yuxi Hu,Wenhao Dong,Jun Zhang,Friedrich Fraundorfer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对锚点式前馈3D-GS在长多视图序列中"表示–融合失配"问题，提出免训练方法CrossPressor，通过覆盖循环证据路由与K/V投影前路由及联合上下文打包，实现可扩展锚点融合，保持接近稠密融合的重建质量，跨视图融合最高加速26.12×。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈3D-GS难以扩展到长多视图序列；ZPressor等锚点式压缩方法虽通过仅从紧凑锚点特征图解码高斯来缓解表示增长，但锚点数量固定，使每个锚点需聚合的支撑视图不断增多，形成表示与融合能力之间的失配。

Method: 免训练的CrossPressor：保留全部面向解码器的锚点查询，对随视图增长的支持序列施加覆盖循环证据路由，将保留的空间证据分散到各支持视图与融合层，并保证所有支持视图均被表示；进一步在K/V投影之前进行路由，并结合联合上下文打包，降低支持侧融合开销且无需重训练。

Result: 重建质量接近稠密融合，跨视图融合最高加速26.12×，长输入序列上端到端加速1.43×。

Conclusion: CrossPressor以无需训练的即插即用方式解决了锚点式前馈3D-GS的表示–融合失配，在几乎不损失重建质量的前提下显著提升长序列的可扩展性与效率。

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D Gaussian Splatting (3D-GS) becomes increasingly difficult to scale to long multi-view sequences. Anchor-based compression methods such as ZPressor alleviate representation growth by decoding Gaussians only from a compact set of anchor feature maps, but keeping these anchors fixed forces each one to aggregate an increasing number of support views. We identify this representation--fusion mismatch and introduce \textbf{CrossPressor}, a training-free method for scalable anchor-based fusion. CrossPressor preserves all decoder-facing anchor queries while constraining the view-growing support sequence through coverage-cycled evidence routing, distributing retained spatial evidence across support views and fusion layers while keeping all support views represented. Routing before K/V projection, together with joint context packing, further reduces support-side fusion cost without retraining. CrossPressor maintains reconstruction quality close to dense fusion while achieving up to $26.12\times$ faster cross-view fusion and $1.43\times$ end-to-end acceleration on long input sequences. Our project page is at: https://yuhsihu.github.io/CrossPressor.

</details>


### [81] [NextGAN: Towards Simple and Scalable GAN Training for Image and Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06270)
*Xinhao Lou,Dongye Xie,Peng Sun,Chenyi Zhuang,Kaicheng Yu,Tao Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NEXTGAN通过将critic的输入梯度回归到冻结表征空间中参考特征与生成特征间的位移，训练标量critic，从而以单一目标将预训练图像/视频生成器蒸馏为1–4步生成器，在ImageNet 256×256上实现1步FID 1.36–2.87，并在Wan2.1-1.3B文本到视频上取得领先的VBench分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规对抗训练只监督critic的标量值，并未显式规定用于更新生成器的梯度方向，导致少步生成训练信号间接且不稳定；同时现有少步蒸馏方法常依赖辅助损失、在线教师分数或轨迹数据，成本高且流程复杂。

Method: 提出NEXTGAN：在冻结表征空间的插值位置上，独立配对参考特征与生成特征，构造二者间的位移向量，并让标量critic的输入梯度回归该位移。理论上证明：对固定生成器，该回归等价于带梯度能量惩罚的对抗目标；而生成器仍采用标准的势函数型GAN更新。方法无需辅助损失、在线教师分数或轨迹，可直接适配最多14B参数的预训练图像与视频生成器。

Result: ImageNet 256×256上，像素空间JiT-B的单步FID为2.87，潜空间DiT-XL/1为1.54，其对抗形式进一步达到1.36。文本到视频方面，单个训练的Wan2.1-1.3B模型同时支持1/2/4步采样，在1步和2步设置下取得对比的1.3B方法中最高的VBench总分；2步得分为83.13，分别超过rCM和AnyFlow 1.25与0.47分。

Conclusion: 显式梯度匹配的critic训练为少步生成提供了更直接、统一的对抗式蒸馏信号，可在不引入额外损失与教师查询的条件下高效适配大规模预训练图像与视频生成器，并在少步FID与VBench指标上取得有竞争力的结果。

Abstract: Adversarial training enables one- and few-step image and video generation, but conventional adversarial losses supervise critic values without directly specifying the gradients used to update the generator. We present NEXTGAN, which trains a scalar critic by regressing its input gradient onto displacements between independently paired reference and generated features at interpolated locations in frozen representation spaces. For a fixed generator, this regression is equivalent to an adversarial objective with a gradient-energy penalty, while the generator retains the standard potential-based GAN update. The same objective adapts pre-trained image and video generators with up to 14B parameters, without auxiliary losses, online teacher scores, or trajectories. On ImageNet $256 \times 256$, NEXTGAN achieves one-step FIDs of 2.87 with pixel-space JiT-B and 1.54 with latent-space DiT-XL/1; its adversarial form further reaches 1.36. For text-to-video generation, a single trained Wan2.1-1.3B model supports one-, two-, and four-step sampling, achieving the highest VBench total scores among the compared 1.3B methods at one and two steps. At two steps, NEXTGAN scores 83.13, exceeding rCM and AnyFlow by 1.25 and 0.47 points, respectively. Code is available at https://github.com/LINs-lab/NextGAN.git.

</details>


### [82] [Quo Vadis, Driving Scene Reconstruction? Occlusion-Aware Gaussian Densification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06273)
*Dung Do Tien,Kien Nguyen Do Trung,Kiet Dang Vu,Trung Thai Tran,Duc Dung Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OccSplat：面向驾驶场景前馈3DGS的遮挡感知高斯稠密化，直接补全3D表示中缺失的几何而非渲染图像像素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈3D Gaussian Splatting仅依赖单次环视观测，输入中被遮挡区域在新视角下会暴露，对应高斯欠约束且不完整；仅在渲染图像空间做修补无法解决几何缺失问题。

Method: 提出前馈框架OccSplat，核心为遮挡感知高斯稠密化：先用Gaussian Occlusion Mapping识别缺乏高斯支撑的遮挡区域，再由Gaussian Occlusion Completion为这些区域生成新的高斯基元，并将其融合进原始表示，保持无需逐场景优化的前馈推理。

Result: 在nuScenes基准上优于对比方法，在遮挡区域增益尤为显著，重建更准确、视觉更连贯。

Conclusion: 将补全从图像空间迁移到3D高斯基元层面，可在保持前馈效率的同时有效缓解单视图观测导致的遮挡不完整问题。

Abstract: While recent feed-forward 3D Gaussian Splatting methods enable efficient driving-scene reconstruction from surround-view images, their reliance on a single observation fundamentally hinders novel-view synthesis. Regions occluded in the input can become visible from novel viewpoints, leaving the reconstructed Gaussians under-constrained and incomplete. Moving beyond image-space inpainting for rendered views, we introduce OccSplat, a feed-forward framework whose core contribution is Occlusion-Aware Gaussian Densification, explicitly targeting missing scene geometry rather than missing pixels. By predicting new Gaussian primitives for unsupported regions, OccSplat completes the scene directly in the 3D representation. Specifically, OccSplat first identifies occluded regions that lack Gaussian support through Gaussian Occlusion Mapping. Gaussian Occlusion Completion then generates new primitives for these regions. The predicted primitives are integrated into the original representation to improve novel-view synthesis while retaining feed-forward inference without per-scene optimization. Experiments on the nuScenes benchmark demonstrate that OccSplat improves on the compared methods and yields particularly large gains in occluded regions, leading to more accurate and visually coherent driving-scene reconstruction.

</details>


### [83] [MonoLaDN: Language-Guided DeNoising for Monocular 3D Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06282)
*Kiet Dang Vu,Cao Le Minh Khoa,Nguyen Viet Tri Pham,An Nguyen Gia,Tam Le Thuc Ho,Duc Dung Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MonoLaDN 通过在训练阶段让冻结的 VLM 生成结构化几何推理并注入带噪查询进行去噪重建，将 VLM 的几何先验蒸馏进纯视觉单目 3D 检测器，推理时不增加任何参数或延迟，在 KITTI 3D 与 nuScenes 上优于 SOTA 且跨域泛化良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目 3D 检测本质上是病态问题（需从单视图推断深度），现有 Transformer 检测器仅从图像特征与 3D 框标注中学习几何，缺乏 VLM 大规模预训练获得的高层语义先验；而直接部署 VLM 作检测器推理开销过大。

Method: 训练时用冻结 VLM 依据图像与 GT 框对每个目标的遮挡、深度、尺寸、角度生成结构化推理；将其编码为查询并加噪扰动，训练检测器重建干净推理，使深度引导特征内化 VLM 几何先验；推理时丢弃推理路径。

Result: 在 KITTI 3D 和 nuScenes 上持续超越 SOTA 检测器，并对新域具有良好泛化性；部署模型无额外延迟与参数。

Conclusion: 语言引导的去噪式先验迁移可在不牺牲推理效率的前提下，为纯视觉单目 3D 检测器注入 VLM 的几何推理能力，是一种有效的"训练期增强、推理期零成本"范式。

Abstract: Monocular 3D object detection offers a low-cost route to 3D perception, but it remains fundamentally ill-posed, since depth must be inferred from a single view. Recent transformer-based detectors learn geometry purely from image features and 3D box annotations, lacking the high-level semantic priors that vision-language models (VLMs) acquire from large-scale pretraining. VLMs offer such priors, but deploying one directly as a detector is impractical due to its heavy inference cost. We propose MonoLaDN, a language-guided denoising framework that transfers the geometric reasoning of a VLM into a monocular 3D detector while keeping the deployed model purely vision-based. During training, a frozen VLM produces structured reasoning about each object's occlusion, depth, dimension, and angle from the image and its ground-truth boxes. We encode this reasoning into queries, perturb them with noise, and train the detector to reconstruct the clean reasoning. Reconstructing the corrupted reasoning forces the detector's depth-guided features to internalize the VLM's geometric priors, so this knowledge remains available even after the reasoning path is discarded at inference, adding no latency or parameters to the deployed model. Experiments on KITTI 3D and nuScenes show that MonoLaDN consistently outperforms state-of-the-art detectors and generalizes well to new domains.

</details>


### [84] [Beyond Scene Shortcuts: Action-Centric Video-Text Alignment for Fine-Grained Action Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06302)
*Zhiliang Zhu,Jiazheng Huang,Dawei Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对细粒度动作理解中的"环境锚定动作幻觉"(EGAH)问题，提出以动作为中心的视频-文本对齐框架STARR，通过动作-环境解耦与注意力一致性奖励强化动作聚焦的视觉定位，构建FineActionR&R数据，基于Qwen2.5-VL-7B训练出FineActionVLM-R1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细粒度动作理解要求模型在时空证据上定位"做什么、如何做、为何做"，但更丰富的监督反而可能带来副作用：微调后的模型倾向于基于静态场景与显著物体生成详细动作描述，而非依据运动本身，产生所谓环境锚定动作幻觉（EGAH），即场景-动作捷径。

Method: 提出STARR（Staged Attention Rectification and Reinforcement）框架：1) 通过动作-环境解耦（Action-Environment Dissociation）打破场景与动作的虚假关联捷径；2) 在GRPO（Group Relative Policy Optimization）中引入注意力一致性奖励（Attention Consistency Reward），强化对动作的聚焦定位。同时构建FineActionR&R数据集，将时间局部化字幕与分层及时序因果推理数据相结合。

Result: 基于Qwen2.5-VL-7B构建的FineActionVLM-R1在MotionBench上平均准确率较基座模型提升3.01个百分点，在HAU-MCQ-Bench上达到91.50%；定性对比显示其细粒度动作描述更忠实，环境诱导的幻觉更少。

Conclusion: 减少场景-动作捷径、显式强化动作聚焦注意力可有效缓解EGAH，说明以动作为中心的对齐与奖励设计对提升视频语言模型的细粒度动作理解具有实效。

Abstract: Fine-grained action understanding requires vision-language models to ground what an agent does, how it acts, and why it acts in spatio-temporal evidence. However, richer supervision can backfire: fine-tuned models may generate detailed action descriptions grounded in static scenes and salient objects rather than motion, a failure we call Environment-Grounded Action Hallucination (EGAH). To mitigate EGAH, we propose Staged Attention Rectification and Reinforcement (STARR), an action-centric video-text alignment framework that breaks scene-action shortcuts through Action-Environment Dissociation and reinforces action-focused grounding with an Attention Consistency Reward within Group Relative Policy Optimization. We further construct FineActionR&R, combining temporally localized captions with hierarchical and temporal-causal reasoning data. Built on Qwen2.5-VL-7B, FineActionVLM-R1 improves average MotionBench accuracy by 3.01 percentage points over its base model and achieves 91.50% on HAU-MCQ-Bench. Qualitative comparisons further indicate more faithful fine-grained action descriptions with fewer environment-induced hallucinations.

</details>


### [85] [Prism: Bidirectional Self-Teaching for Unified Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06343)
*Peng Zhang,Qian Chen,Xianyin Zhang,Yimin Tian,Ruite Fei,Dongyi Lv,Lifan Guo,Chi Zhang,Shuai Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Prism：一种面向统一多模态模型的双向自教学后训练方法，将自生成交错轨迹解耦为文本链与图像链并依据其可靠性构建训练目标，实现理解与生成的协同提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有UMM后训练方法存在局限：或将理解作为固定教师单向提升生成，或依赖真实轨迹同时监督理解与生成，缺乏双向且不依赖外部校验的自监督信号来源。

Method: 从模型自生成的交错rollout中解耦出文本链和图像链，分别评估两条链的可靠性，并依据可靠性状态构造对应的训练目标，从而获得双向监督（生成→理解、理解→生成）。

Result: 在SenseNova-U1上生成端GenEval由85.8升至89.2（+3.4）、DPG由86.1升至87.3（+1.2）；理解端MME总分由2282.6升至2311.6、MMVP由58.0升至62.0；在BAGEL上取得一致改进。

Conclusion: 通过可靠性引导、从自生成轨迹构建自训练目标，Prism无需外部验证器或人工标注的修正轨迹，即可实现生成与理解的双向协同增益。

Abstract: Unified Multimodal Models (UMMs) aim to support visual understanding and generation within a single model. Existing post-training methods either use understanding as a fixed teacher to improve generation, or rely on ground-truth trajectories to supervise both understanding and generation. Prism instead derives bidirectional supervision from self-generated interleaved rollouts, constructing training targets according to the reliability of their text and image chains. We propose Prism, a bidirectional self-teaching post-training method for UMMs. Like a prism decomposing mixed light, Prism decouples a coupled interleaved trajectory into a text chain and an image chain, assesses the reliability of each chain, and constructs a corresponding training target from their reliability states. Experiments on both SenseNova-U1 and BAGEL show that Prism improves both understanding and generation. On the generation side, GenEval improves from 85.8 to 89.2 (+3.4), and DPG improves from 86.1 to 87.3 (+1.2) on SenseNova-U1. On the understanding side, the total MME score improves from 2282.6 to 2311.6, and MMVP improves from 58.0 to 62.0. Consistent improvements are observed on BAGEL. These results highlight that Prism enables coordinated improvements in generation and understanding by constructing reliability-guided self-training targets from self-generated trajectories, without relying on an external verifier or externally annotated corrected trajectories during target construction.

</details>


### [86] [Traffic-VL: Reinforced Vision-Language Models for Generalizable End-to-End Traffic Signal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06353)
*Xingchen Zou,Maonan Wang,Kemou Jiang,Jie Li,Yiqi Chen,Xi Chen,Hai Yang,Yuxuan Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SynTra-V（物理仿真到逼真多视角路侧监控图像的数据生成+语义审计）与Traffic-VL（80亿参数VLM，直接从多视角图像与自然语言相位描述输出信号决策），弥合视觉域与感知-决策鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有信号控制方法依赖专用感知管线产出的结构化交通状态；视觉方法多用理想化仿真图像且需先转中间表征，难以直接利用路侧视觉观测。需同时弥合"视觉域差距"与"感知到决策差距"。

Method: SynTra-V将基于物理的交通仿真转换为逼真的多视角监控图像，覆盖多种道路拓扑与传感条件，并用自动语义审计校验控制关键几何、车辆运动与车道语义与仿真状态一致；Traffic-VL基于Traffic-R1两阶段强化推理框架，先将多视角图像与自然语言相位描述直接映射为信号决策（无中间结构化状态），视觉编码器在SynTra-V上做离线强化学习适配，随后在闭环仿真的在线视觉逐步轨迹策略优化中冻结。

Result: Traffic-VL达到与结构化状态Traffic-R1相当的性能，优于图像型控制器与通用VLM（含Gemini 3 Pro）；在未见拓扑、退化传感条件、真实监控视频及十路口三周部署中表现出稳健的视觉泛化与有效的受限在线控制。

Conclusion: 端到端视觉-语言信号控制可行：逼真可审计的仿真数据生成加两阶段强化推理，可使VLM直接从路侧视觉观测决策，并具备真实场景泛化与部署有效性。

Abstract: Existing traffic signal control methods typically rely on structured traffic states produced by dedicated perception pipelines. Emerging visual approaches often use idealized simulator images and convert them into intermediate representations before control, limiting their ability to directly exploit roadside visual observations. To bridge both the visual-domain and perception-to-decision gaps, we introduce SynTra-V and Traffic-VL. SynTra-V converts physics-based traffic simulations into photorealistic multi-view surveillance imagery across diverse road topologies and sensing conditions, and automatic semantic auditing checks that control-critical geometry, vehicle movements, and lane semantics match the simulator state. Traffic-VL is an eight-billion-parameter vision-language model that directly maps synchronized multi-view images and natural-language phase descriptions to signal decisions without intermediate structured states. Building on Traffic-R1's two-stage reinforced reasoning framework, Traffic-VL adapts its vision encoder via offline reinforcement learning on SynTra-V and freezes it during online visual stepwise trajectory policy optimization in closed-loop simulation. Experiments show that Traffic-VL matches structured-state Traffic-R1 while outperforming image-based controllers and general-purpose vision-language models, including Gemini 3 Pro. Evaluations on unseen topologies, degraded sensing conditions, real surveillance footage, and a three-week deployment across ten intersections further demonstrate robust visual generalization and effective constrained online control.

</details>


### [87] [FlashRelight: Portrait Video Relighting with Dynamic Lighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06355)
*Jiahui Sheng,Le Li,Limin Lin,Jiahao Li,Wansen Feng,Hong Gu,Shuhan Chen,Xiaorun Li,Rui Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlashRelight：两阶段动态人像视频重打光框架，先去光照再合成动态光照，支持环境贴图序列、动态光照文本、光照参考视频三种控制模态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态光照下（方向、强度、颜色随时间变化）的人像视频重打光极具挑战，需同时保证真实感与时间一致性。

Method: 采用两阶段框架：第一阶段通过 delighting 抑制源光照，得到光照中性的人像视频；第二阶段在该视频上合成目标动态光照。支持三种互补控制模态：环境贴图序列（逐帧显式光照控制）、动态光照文本（语义光照编辑）、光照参考视频（视觉光照迁移）。训练采用低成本混合策略，结合静态单光源逐次（OLAT）数据与生成的动态光照人像视频，兼顾受控光照变化与真实人像运动。

Result: 大量实验表明，FlashRelight 实现时间连贯、照片级真实的动态重打光，并支持灵活的跨视频光照迁移。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 通过去光照—重光照的解耦两阶段设计与混合训练策略，FlashRelight 有效解决动态人像视频重打光难题，并提供多模态光照控制能力。

Abstract: Portrait video relighting aims to modify the illumination of a portrait video while preserving photorealism and temporal stability. The task becomes particularly challenging under dynamic lighting, where illumination direction, intensity, and color vary over time. To tackle this problem, we introduce FlashRelight, a novel two-stage framework for dynamic portrait video relighting. FlashRelight first suppresses the source illumination through delighting, and then synthesizes the target dynamic lighting on an illumination-neutral portrait video. FlashRelight supports three complementary control modalities: envmap sequences for explicit frame-level lighting control, dynamic lighting text for semantic illumination editing, and lighting reference videos for visual lighting transfer. Training follows a low-cost hybrid strategy that combines static one-light-at-a-time (OLAT) data with generated dynamic-lighting portrait videos, covering both controlled illumination variation and realistic portrait motion. Extensive experiments show that FlashRelight achieves temporally coherent and photorealistic dynamic relighting while enabling flexible cross-video lighting transfer. Code is available at https://github.com/vivoCameraResearch/FlashRelight

</details>


### [88] [NanoKV: High-Ratio KV Cache Compression for Autoregressive Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06377)
*Jiacheng Lu,Haoyi Zhu,Sipei Yi,Enze Xie,Cheng Zhuo,Yu Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NanoKV 是一种冻结骨干的生成感知高倍率 KV 压缩框架，利用跨 token 与注意力头的冗余，并针对 Key/Value 在注意力中的不同角色采用不同拟合目标，实现高质量、高压缩率及可选的计算加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频模型依赖历史 KV 缓存以在长时 rollout 中保持场景一致性，但 KV 存储与对历史重复注意力成为不断增长的内存与计算瓶颈；激进压缩会损害生成质量，而重建全维 KV 又几乎不减少注意力计算量，因此需要兼顾生成质量与存储/计算效率的压缩方案。

Method: 提出 NanoKV：冻结视频骨干，将跨 token 与注意力头的共享内容存入聚类中心，并把联合的头-通道残差编码为低位系数。关键点是按 Key/Value 在注意力中的不同作用使用不同拟合目标：Key 解码器以保留下游块输出为优化目标，Value 编码器/解码器则通过实际部署的量化路径进行保真重建拟合。在此压缩表示之上，可选变体 NanoKV-Fast 复用存储系数做混合维度注意力，在不改动骨干的前提下降低计算。

Result: 在三个自回归视频骨干上一致改善质量-存储权衡。在 HY-WorldPlay 上实现 26.09 倍有效 KV 压缩，约为 KVTC 的两倍，同时 PSNR 提升 1.0 dB；NanoKV-Fast 保持 20.53 倍有效压缩，并相对 KVTC 获得约 1.15 倍端到端加速。

Conclusion: NanoKV 通过聚类共享表示结合角色差异化的 Key/Value 拟合目标，实现了生成质量与高倍率压缩的良好权衡，并可在同一压缩表示上进一步支持混合维度注意力以降低计算，具有良好的通用性与可扩展性。

Abstract: Autoregressive video models rely on historical key-value (KV) caches to preserve scene consistency over extended rollouts, making KV storage and repeated attention over history a growing memory and computational bottleneck. Aggressive compression can degrade generation quality, while reconstructing full-dimensional KV leaves attention computation largely unchanged. We introduce NanoKV, a frozen-backbone framework for generation-aware high-ratio KV compression. NanoKV exploits redundancy across tokens and attention heads by storing shared content in cluster centers and encoding joint head-channel residuals into low-bit coefficients. Crucially, NanoKV uses different fitting objectives for Keys and Values according to their distinct roles in attention: Key decoders are optimized to preserve downstream block outputs, whereas Value encoders and decoders are fitted for faithful reconstruction through the deployed quantization path. Building on the same compressed representation, the optional NanoKV-Fast variant reuses the stored coefficients for mixed-dimensional attention, reducing computation without modifying the video backbone. Across three autoregressive video backbones, NanoKV consistently improves the quality-storage trade-off. On HY-WorldPlay, NanoKV achieves $26.09\times$ effective KV compression, nearly twice that of KVTC, while improving PSNR by 1.0 dB. NanoKV-Fast retains $20.53\times$ effective compression and achieves an approximately $1.15\times$ end-to-end speedup over KVTC.

</details>


### [89] [A Bag of Tricks for Defending Deep Learning-based Face Recognition Pipelines against Backdoor Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06412)
*Quentin Le Roux,Marti Jimenez,Yannick Teglia,Philippe Loubet Moundi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该技术报告针对“训练数据来自可能被攻陷的第三方、但训练在内部完成”的威胁模型，提出三种防御数据投毒后门攻击的实用技巧：一种训练时检测被投毒身份，另两种在推理时过滤可疑输入，并在真实人脸识别负载上评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成熟的多阶段人脸识别系统需被保护，而工业界实践中训练数据常源自可能被攻陷的第三方，使基于数据投毒的后门攻击成为最相关的威胁；现有研究多假设端到端受控，缺乏面向系统提供者的可落地加固方案。

Method: 提出三种防御“trick”：第一种在训练阶段，通过检测在训练中/部署前识别出被投毒身份；后两种在推理阶段，通过过滤可疑输入在返回答案前阻断潜在攻击者。每种方法均给出动机、算法与计算开销分析。

Result: 三种方法均在真实人脸识别工作负载上进行了评估，报告给出了其有效性与计算影响（摘要未提供具体量化指标）。

Conclusion: 该报告为系统提供者提供可操作的加固技术，覆盖训练时与推理时两个环节以抵御数据投毒后门攻击。

Abstract: The protection of mature, multi-stage Face Recognition Systems is crucial to system providers. This technical report targets the threat model most relevant to industry practitioners: although model training is performed in-house, training data is sourced from possibly-compromised third parties. Given this setting, we present three practical defense "tricks" against data poisoning-based backdoor attacks. The first operates at training-time, detecting poisoned identities during training or prior to a pipeline's deployment. The last two operate at inference-time, filtering suspicious inputs before an answer can be sent back to a potential attacker. Each method is presented with their motivation, algorithms and computational impact, and evaluated on realistic face recognition workloads. Our goal is to provide actionable hardening techniques to system providers.

</details>


### [90] [Entropogram-guided Visual Realignment Decoding: Capturing the Rhythm of Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06415)
*Jiaqi Deng,Zhe Fu,Yakun Chen,Dichang Zhang,Zonghan Wu,Xiaoshui Huang,Huan Huo,Guandong Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文从解码过程的熵动态视角研究多模态大模型的幻觉，识别出两种与幻觉相关的熵模式，并提出熵感知的自我反思框架，在检测到可疑幻觉状态时进行视觉重对齐，在六个基准的九个设置上提升推理准确性与抗幻觉鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM在跨模态推理任务上进展显著，但在长链推理中仍存在幻觉。作者认为熵不应仅被视为静态不确定性度量，而是反映解码过程中模态信息如何被整合的结构化信号，因此希望从推理生成动态的角度重新审视幻觉成因。

Method: 基于对熵动态的系统分析，作者提出熵感知的自我反思框架：在解码过程中持续追踪熵的演化，当检测到可疑的幻觉状态时，执行针对性的视觉重对齐，促使模型在关键推理阶段重新审视显著的视觉证据。

Result: 分析发现两种与幻觉相关的特征熵模式：(1) 整体高置信区间对应过早收敛到不恰当的多模态交互；(2) 局部熵的突变与推理不稳定及幻觉发生相关联。在覆盖九个评估设置的六个多模态推理基准上，方法在推理准确性与幻觉鲁棒性上均取得稳健提升。

Conclusion: 熵动态可作为一种有效的幻觉诊断信号，基于此的自我反思式视觉重对齐机制能够缓解MLLM长链推理中的幻觉，并为理解其生成动态下的失败机理提供了新视角。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have demonstrated remarkable progress on cross-modal reasoning tasks, yet they continue to suffer from hallucinations in long-chain reasoning processes. In this work, we revisit hallucination in MLLMs from the perspective of reasoning generation dynamics. We argue that entropy is not merely a static measure of uncertainty but a structured signal that reflects how modality information is integrated throughout decoding. Through systematic analysis, we identify two characteristic entropy patterns associated with hallucination behaviors: (1) a generally high-confidence regime corresponds to premature convergence to inappropriate multimodal interactions; and (2) local abrupt entropy transitions are associated with reasoning instability and hallucination onset. Building on these observations, we propose an entropy-aware self-introspective framework for MLLM reasoning. Our method continuously tracks entropy dynamics during decoding and performs targeted visual re-alignment when suspicious hallucination states are detected, encouraging the model to re-examine salient visual evidence at critical reasoning stages. Extensive experiments across six multimodal reasoning benchmarks spanning nine distinct evaluation settings demonstrate that our approach delivers robust improvements in reasoning accuracy and hallucination robustness, while providing new insights into the generation dynamics underlying MLLM failures. The code is available at: https://github.com/jden4524/SelfRetrospectiveDecoding.

</details>


### [91] [Unaligned Image Guided Denoising via Cross-modal Conditional Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06422)
*Runmin Zhang,Linshan Li,Si-Yuan Cao,Zhu Yu,Lingyu Zhu,Huaqi Zhang,Hui-Liang Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于跨模态条件流匹配的非对齐图像引导去噪框架UGD-FM，在强噪声与大范围二维跨模态错位并存条件下实现少步高质量去噪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有引导去噪方法多假设空间对齐输入或仅处理小基线校正图像对，无法应对强目标噪声与大范围二维跨模态错位共存的困难场景。

Method: 将引导去噪建模为渐进式流匹配过程，使图像复原与对齐引导聚合相互促进；引入聚焦关键去噪阶段、与推理一致的训练策略以支持少步推理并缓解训练-推理失配；提出自适应稀疏引导传播实现高效可靠的大范围匹配，并用感知与几何辅助损失进行正则化。

Result: 实验表明UGD-FM仅需一至两步推理即达到最先进去噪性能，且兼容现代单图复原骨干网络。

Conclusion: 流匹配框架可有效统一非对齐引导匹配与去噪复原，并在少步推理下取得SOTA表现，具备良好骨干兼容性。

Abstract: This work presents UGD-FM, a novel unaligned image guided denoising framework based on cross-modal conditional flow matching. Unlike previous approaches that assume spatially aligned inputs or only handle small-baseline rectified pairs, we address a more challenging setting where severe target noise and large-range two-dimensional cross-modal misalignment coexist. Specifically, we formulate guided denoising as a progressive flow matching process, allowing image restoration and aligned guidance aggregation to mutually reinforce each other. To support efficient few-step inference, we introduce a stage-focused and inference-consistent training strategy that focuses on task-critical denoising stages, and mitigates the training-inference mismatch. We further propose adaptive sparse guidance propagation for efficient and reliable large-range matching, regularized by perceptual and geometric auxiliary losses. Experiments demonstrate that UGD-FM achieves state-of-the-art denoising performance with only one or two inference steps, and is compatible with modern single-image restoration backbones.

</details>


### [92] [DreamRealm: Exploring and Materializing Hallucination Worlds for Trustworthy Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06433)
*Benlei Cui,Chunyang Chai,Yiliang Zhang,Xiaoqian Xia,Chengwen Yao,Song Li,Longtao Huang,Yuwen Zhai,Haiwen Hong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DreamRealm后训练框架，通过挖掘模型潜在幻觉并用图像最小编辑将其"实现"，再用RealmMatch匹配学习，提升细粒度视觉感知并缓解幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通过图像检索/生成/编辑构建视觉对比的后训练方法，并非围绕模型自身的幻觉构建，难以保证监督信号对准其真实感知混淆；另一类响应纠正或偏好学习方法只优化图像固定的输出侧，忽视了图像输入侧的监督信号设计。

Method: DreamRealm框架：(1) RealmForgePipe数据构建流水线，从目标模型中挖掘已观测幻觉与潜在幻觉，并通过图像编辑将其物质化；(2) 基于Qwen3.5-9B构造Realized Hallucination Dataset (RealmSet)；(3) RealmMatch训练目标，在图像-响应组合上构建似然得分矩阵，结合监督学习与双向对比损失，强化正确匹配、抑制错误匹配。

Result: 相较原始Qwen3.5-9B，在六个视觉感知与幻觉基准上均有提升，其中HallusionBench准确率提升7.26个百分点，MMVP配对准确率提升11.34个百分点。

Conclusion: 消融实验验证了数据构建与训练方法的有效性，表明"幻觉归属（hallucination assignment）"是提升视觉感知、缓解MLLM视觉幻觉的有效范式。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) remain prone to visual hallucinations when correct responses depend on subtle visual details. One line of post-training methods constructs visual contrasts through image retrieval, generation, or editing to improve fine-grained perception. However, these contrasts are not built around the model's own hallucinations, making it difficult to ensure that supervision targets its actual perceptual confusions. Another line suppresses hallucinated outputs through response correction or preference learning under fixed image conditions, but leaves the design of supervision signals on the image-input side underexplored. Inspired by how humans correct misconceptions, we introduce DreamRealm, a post-training framework that explores diverse latent hallucinated responses, minimally edits original images to make them valid, and trains the model to assign each response to its supporting image. Our data construction pipeline, RealmForgePipe, mines observed and latent hallucinations from a target model and materializes them through image editing. Using hallucinations mined from Qwen3.5-9B, we construct the Realized Hallucination Dataset (RealmSet). Our training objective, RealmMatch, constructs a likelihood score matrix across image--response combinations and combines supervised learning with bidirectional contrastive losses to reinforce correct matches and suppress mismatches. Compared with the original Qwen3.5-9B model, DreamRealm improves performance across six visual perception and hallucination benchmarks, including gains of 7.26 percentage points in HallusionBench accuracy and 11.34 percentage points in MMVP pair accuracy. Together with ablation studies on data construction and training methods, these results demonstrate that hallucination assignment is a promising paradigm for improving visual perception and mitigating hallucinations.

</details>


### [93] [Within-Family EfficientNet Scaling under Fixed-Resolution Transfer Learning for Spectral-Domain Optical Coherence Tomography Retinal Disease Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06452)
*Shkelqim Sherifi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 评估 EfficientNet-B0至B7在224×224固定分辨率下对SD-OCT四分类的性能，发现精度随模型规模非单调变化（B0 95.93%至B1 96.95%，B7 96.73%），更大模型并未稳定提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CNN在SD-OCT视网膜疾病分类中表现良好，但EfficientNet基于自然图像的缩放层级能否迁移到固定分辨率下的医学图像迁移学习尚不明确，本文旨在考察该缩放规律及精度-效率权衡。

Method: 在Kermany数据集上用8个ImageNet预训练的EfficientNet变体（4.05–64.10M骨干参数），输入224×224、灰度复制为RGB，采用模型内置预处理与增强；训练分冻结骨干的头部训练与部分微调两阶段，评估分类、判别、效率及可视化指标。

Result: 测试精度非单调变化：B0 95.93%、B1 96.95%（最高）、B7 96.73%；B1的Drusen F1最高（0.8780），B7的Drusen召回最高（0.9177）；Drusen在所有变体F1最低，占各类F1平方偏差总和的82.2%，类别排序恒为Normal>CNV>DME>Drusen；精度/参数比由19.82降至1.48个百分点每百万参数；训练-测试差距与测试精度关联弱且不显著（r=0.26，p=0.53）。

Conclusion: 更大EfficientNet变体并未持续提升SD-OCT分类性能；B1精度与Drusen F1最优，B7在大规模下出现次级精度回升但CPU延迟更高，说明缩放效应依赖任务与训练协议；因跨分区重复样本未经独立审计，结论以所给数据划分为条件。

Abstract: Background/Objectives: CNNs perform well in retinal disease classification from spectral-domain optical coherence tomography (SD-OCT), but whether EfficientNet's natural-image scaling hierarchy transfers to medical-image transfer learning at fixed resolution remains unclear. This study evaluates EfficientNet-B0-B7 for four-class SD-OCT classification and accuracy-efficiency trade-offs. Methods: Eight ImageNet-pretrained variants (4.05-64.10M instantiated backbone parameters) were evaluated at 224x224 on the Kermany dataset. Grayscale images were replicated to RGB and processed using model-embedded preprocessing and augmentation. Training comprised frozen-backbone head training followed by partial fine-tuning. Metrics covered classification, discrimination, efficiency, and visual analyses. Results: Test accuracy varied non-monotonically from 95.93% (B0) to 96.95% (B1); B7 achieved 96.73%. B1 had the highest accuracy and Drusen F1 (0.8780), while B7 had Drusen F1 of 0.8720 and the highest Drusen recall (0.9177). Drusen had the lowest F1 across all variants and contributed 82.2% of the summed squared deviation in class-specific F1; class ranking remained Normal > CNV > DME > Drusen. The OCT accuracy ranking differed from the reported ImageNet hierarchy, where B7 ranked first. The accuracy-to-parameter ratio fell from 19.82 to 1.48 percentage points per million total parameters. The final train-test gap had a weak, non-significant association with test accuracy (r = 0.26, p = 0.53). Conclusions: Larger EfficientNet variants did not consistently improve SD-OCT classification. B1 achieved the highest accuracy and Drusen F1, whereas B7 showed a secondary accuracy recovery at greater model size and CPU latency, indicating task and protocol dependent scaling effects. As cross-partition duplicates have not been independently audited, results are conditional on the supplied split.

</details>


### [94] [CoDecide: Content-Adaptive Video Decoding for High-Throughput Video Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06455)
*Shristy Shah,Thomas B. Moeslund,Kamal Nasrollahi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CoDecide是一种回顾式视频分析系统，利用压缩码流信号仅解码“出现新内容”的帧，通过冻结DINOv2教师的无标签新颖性信号蒸馏轻量压缩域学生模型，实现在等解码预算下优于现有压缩域选择器并快15–24倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频档案分析受解码瓶颈制约：逐帧检测时解码占比小，但一旦仅在选定帧上运行检测器，解码便成为主要开销。现有运动信号只能说明“有运动”，无法区分物体是新进入场景还是长期存在，因此需要一种能识别语义新颖性的帧选择机制。

Method: 以压缩码流中的运动信号为入口，用冻结DINOv2教师产生的无标签语义新颖性信号监督一个轻量压缩域学生模型，使其对语义新区域而非任意运动响应；推理时由学生模型决定解码哪些帧、重建哪些区域，并用运动矢量将已解码帧的检测结果传播到跳过帧。

Result: 在VIRAT、MEVA、ShanghaiTech上采用留一数据集出协议评估：相同解码预算下，CoDecide恢复的运动目标检测数多于先前的压缩域选择器，甚至优于直接在目标相机上训练的对比方法；相比逐帧解码+检测的流水线，加速15–24倍。

Conclusion: 通过将语义新颖性引入压缩域帧选择，可实现无需逐相机重训练的跨部署泛化，为视频档案分析提供高性价比的回顾式解码与检测方案。

Abstract: Analytics on video archives are bottlenecked by decoding. In a dense pipeline that runs a detector on every frame, decoding is a small fraction of the runtime, but once the detector is restricted to selected frames, decoding takes over as the main cost. We introduce CoDecide, a retrospective analytics system that only decodes the frames where new content appears, using signals from the compressed bitstream to find them. Motion tells us something is moving, but not whether it is new to the scene or has been there the whole time. To make that distinction, we supervise a lightweight compressed-domain student on a label-free novelty signal from a frozen DINOv2 teacher, which fires on semantically new regions rather than on any motion. Consequently, the student model generalizes to new camera deployments without requiring per-camera retraining. At inference, it decides which frames to decode and which regions to reconstruct. Detections from the decoded frames are propagated to the skipped frames using motion vectors. We evaluate CoDecide with a leave-one-dataset-out protocol on VIRAT, MEVA, and ShanghaiTech. Against a dense per-frame detector, CoDecide recovers more moving-object detections than prior compressed-domain selectors at an equivalent decoding budget, outperforming even a competitor trained directly on the target camera. CoDecide runs 15 to 24x faster than a pipeline that decodes and detects every frame. Code is available at https://github.com/shristy51/CoDecide.

</details>


### [95] [A 3D foundation model unifying CT interpretation and segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06471)
*Yulu Xie,Zilong Li,Shen Xu,Yifan Bai,Liuhong Shi,Jiao Chen,Xianglei Kong,Wenchao Zhu,Na Shen,Xianghua Ye,Yang Yan,Jiaju Zheng,Yunsong Peng,Yanzhou Su,Chaohui Yu,Hao Luo,Fan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出通用3D CT基础模型HoloCT，将CT解读与体素级分割统一建模，通过语言-空间双向协同在多任务和多中心验证中超越专家与现有基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 放射科医生解读CT时同时判断"发现是什么"与"位于三维解剖何处"，但现有CT模型将解读与3D分割割裂，导致文本发现缺乏空间可验证性，也未能利用临床理解与空间感知之间的协同潜力。

Method: 构建HoloCT基础模型，联合建模全卷上下文与细粒度解剖细节，以3D掩码作为证据输出；构建HoloDATA数据集（73个公开数据集与6个临床队列共195,921例CT、620万条指令对话、280万对CT-掩码）。

Result: 在61个内部与12个外部数据集、346项任务（分割、病灶筛查、VQA、报告生成）中优于专科模型与基础模型；五家医院11,455例多中心验证中，病灶分割与报告生成分别领先18.2与15.3分；甲状腺肿瘤与结肠病灶筛查平均准确率85.1%，回顾性阅片研究中超过全部四位参与放射科医生；语言引导分割领先22.1分，生成3D掩码亦显著提升解读性能。

Conclusion: 临床理解与空间感知可在单一基础模型内联合学习，语义-空间存在双向协同，为更准确可靠的CT分析提供了路径。

Abstract: Radiologists interpret computed tomography (CT) scans by jointly determining what a finding represents and where it lies in three-dimensional (3D) anatomy. However, CT models have treated CT interpretation and 3D segmentation as separate tasks, leaving textual findings spatially unverifiable and overlooking potential synergy between clinical understanding and spatial perception. Here we present HoloCT, a generalist 3D foundation model unifying CT interpretation and voxel-level segmentation by jointly modelling whole-volume context and fine-grained anatomical detail, providing 3D masks as evidence. We curated HoloDATA, comprising 195,921 CT scans from 73 public datasets and six clinical cohorts, with 6.2 million instruction dialogues and 2.8 million CT-mask pairs. Across 346 tasks spanning segmentation, lesion screening, visual question answering, and report generation on 61 internal and 12 external datasets, HoloCT outperformed specialist and foundation models. In multicentre validation across 11,455 clinical CT scans from five hospitals, HoloCT outperformed competing models in lesion segmentation and report generation by 18.2 and 15.3 points. It also achieved 85.1% mean accuracy in screening thyroid tumours and colon lesions, exceeding all four participating radiologists in a retrospective reader study. Further analyses revealed bidirectional semantic-spatial synergy: language-guided segmentation delineated unseen anatomical structures and lesions as well as diffuse free-text findings, outperforming the leading approach by 22.1 points, whereas prompting the model to generate corresponding 3D masks alongside its textual outputs significantly improved interpretation performance. HoloCT shows that clinical understanding and spatial perception can be learned together within a single foundation model and offers a path towards more accurate and reliable CT analysis.

</details>


### [96] [EmoReward: Self-Evolving Reward Calibration for Emotional Video Generation RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06518)
*Mingzhe Zheng,Yue Wu,Jing Tan,Chang Zou,Dengyang Jiang,Zeyue Tian,Xuanhua He,Ryan Xu,Yunyang Ge,Sihui Ji,Yunlong Lin,Kaihang Pan,Hongru Yan,Sixiang Chen,Dianyi Wang,Julian Zhang,Weijie Kong,Changlin Li,Lanqing Guo,Zhao Zhong,Qifeng Chen,Harry Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EmoReward：一种自进化训练机制，从实际训练运行的奖励轨迹中蒸馏证据可靠性，自适应决定多奖励权重分配，用于视频生成的情感表达后训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频质量（尤其情感表达）评估依赖主观美学、无唯一正确目标；现有固定奖励（视觉质量、运动质量、文本对齐）难以捕捉情感意图且易引发奖励作弊（reward hacking）；RLHF 难以扩展，个体审美差异使面部、身体运动与色彩线索的校准在叙事语境下复杂化。

Method: 1）先从情感提示中规划具体 grounding cues 指导评估；2）Act 阶段用异构信号原语对生成视频打分；3）Reflective Reward Gating 通过实例级门控（阶段、人脸置信度、冲突、微妙性）校准各原语贡献，并在活跃集上重归一化得到样本融合奖励；4）RewardMemory 在训练运行结束后按情感区制从 rollout 分数与结果估计各原语可靠性，固定有界乘子以校准下一次训练的融合权重，且不更新奖励模型。

Result: 在 HunyuanVideo 1.5 上跨多种 RL 算法取得提升，并在 SAGE-GRPO 下与其他 agentic reward 方法对比；在 SD 3.5 与 FLUX 上的消融与迁移实验进一步支持自适应奖励校准对视觉生成的有效性。

Conclusion: 以"训练轨迹驱动的可靠性蒸馏"替代固定奖励，可在不更新奖励模型的前提下实现可扩展、可校准的情感感知奖励分配，并具备跨模型迁移性。

Abstract: Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) has advanced generative model post-training in verifiable domains such as code and mathematics, but evaluating video quality, especially emotional expression, often depends on subjective aesthetics and has no uniquely correct target. Existing approaches typically rely on fixed rewards for visual quality, motion quality, and text alignment that poorly capture emotional intent and can invite reward hacking, while RLHF is difficult to scale and subject to individual aesthetic differences that complicate the calibration of facial, body motion, and chromatic cues across narrative contexts. We introduce EmoReward, a new self-evolving training mechanism that distills evidence reliability from reward traces collected during an actual training run to adaptively guide multi-reward weight allocation in subsequent training by determining which emotional evidence to trust and how much to weight it. EmoReward first plans specific grounding cues from the emotional prompt to guide evaluation, then uses heterogeneous signal primitives to score the generated video during the act stage. Reflective Reward Gating calibrates their contributions through instance-level gates for phase, face confidence, conflict, and subtlety, then renormalizes weights over the active set to produce the sample's fused reward. RewardMemory then estimates each primitive's reliability from rollout scores and outcomes within each emotional regime in the finished training run, then fixes bounded multipliers that calibrate fusion weights in the next training run without updating reward models. Experiments on HunyuanVideo 1.5 show improvements across multiple RL algorithms and compare EmoReward with other agentic reward methods under SAGE-GRPO. Ablations and transfer experiments on SD 3.5 and FLUX further support adaptive reward calibration for visual generation.

</details>


### [97] [Spatially and Compositionally Controlled Multimodal Glioblastoma MRI Synthesis with Latent Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06540)
*Steven Glass,Max Van Puyvelde,Marija Pizurica,Mia Meyer,Olivier Gevaert,Willie Brink*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出两阶段潜在扩散模型框架，可控生成胶质母细胞瘤2D MRI图像：先按位置/大小/层面/亚区组成生成分割图，再以ControlNet按分割生成四种模态图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胶质母细胞瘤在MRI各模态间形态与组成高度异质，临床数据集在病理分布上严重不平衡，需可控合成数据以增强/平衡数据。

Method: 两阶段LDM：第一阶段为分割LDM，以位置、空间范围、轴位层面、亚区组成为条件生成肿瘤图；第二阶段为模态条件LDM+ControlNet，依据指定分割生成T1、T1Gd、T2、FLAIR图像。

Result: 留出测试集上生成分割质心误差0.842像素、亚区比例误差0.795个百分点；ControlNet在四模态均降低FID，合并FID由42.59降至36.12；专家评分：真实6.60、ControlNet生成6.83、全合成6.77。

Conclusion: 该框架可实现显式可控、模态一致的胶质母细胞瘤MRI合成，分割与图像质量指标及专家评审均表明其真实感可与真实图像相当。

Abstract: Glioblastoma exhibits substantial heterogeneity in tumour morphology and composition across MRI modalities, often making available clinical datasets highly unbalanced in terms of patient pathologies. We propose a two-stage latent diffusion model (LDM) framework for the generation of explicitly controllable 2D glioblastoma MRI scans. First, a segmentation LDM generates tumour maps conditioned on location, spatial extent, axial position, and subregion composition. Second, a modality-conditioned LDM with ControlNet generates T1, T1Gd, T2, and FLAIR images adhering to the requested segmentation. On a held-out test set, generated segmentations achieved a mean centroid error of 0.842 pixels and a subregion proportion error of 0.795 percentage points. ControlNet improved Fréchet inception distance (FID) across all four modalities, reducing pooled FID from 42.59 to 36.12. An expert reader study yielded mean quality ratings of 6.60 for real images, 6.83 for ControlNet-generated images, and 6.77 for fully synthetic images.

</details>


### [98] [MKD2World: A Typed Spatial MarkDown World Model For Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06544)
*Zhihao Nan,Sifan Zhou,Yiming Cheng,Jinkai Zheng,Zheng Zhu,Si Shi,Ming Li,Fei Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Typed Spatial Markdown (TSM)状态表示与MKD2World桌面网页世界模型，用于GUI智能体的前瞻式决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GUI智能体借助世界模型前瞻可提升决策，但前提是预测状态需准确反映动作后的界面。现有状态表示无法同时捕获界面布局、UI语义与显式位置信息。

Method: 1) TSM：Markdown风格文本状态表示，按空间关系序列化块级与原子级UI元素，联合编码类型、视觉语义、显式坐标、可选控件状态及层级归属；2) AtomBlock-WebUI数据集：通过向HTML注入元素类型标签、渲染后直接提取边界框，避免事后推断带来的标注误差，提供双粒度精确标注；3) MKD2World：两阶段训练的网页世界模型，分别做当前状态描述与动作条件下的下一状态预测；推理时智能体按需查询并执行“digest”步骤提取任务相关证据以选动作。

Result: MKD2World-9B在域外测试集EleF1达26.50，超过GPT-5.6-Sol(14.98)共11.52点；在Online-Mind2Web上将Qwen3.5-35B智能体成功率由22.94%提升至37.61%，在开源GUI世界模型中具竞争力。

Conclusion: 结构化、带类型的空间文本表示结合两阶段动作条件世界建模，可有效提升桌面网页智能体的状态预测精度与任务成功率。

Abstract: Graphical user interface (GUI) agents can improve decision-making with world-model foresight, provided that predicted states accurately reflect post-action interfaces. Existing state representations fail to jointly capture interface layout, UI semantics, and explicit locations. We introduce Typed Spatial Markdown (TSM), a Markdown-style textual state representation for desktop web interfaces. TSM serializes block-level and atomic-level UI elements according to their spatial relationships, jointly encoding their types, visual semantics, explicit locations, optional control states, and hierarchical membership. To train a screenshot-to-TSM renderer, we construct AtomBlock-WebUI, a dataset with precise annotations at both granularities. By injecting element-type labels into HTML and directly extracting bounding boxes after rendering, we avoid annotation errors caused by post-hoc inference from webpage structures. Building on TSM, we propose MKD2World, a desktop web world model trained in two stages for current-state description and action-conditioned next-state prediction. During inference, the web agent queries MKD2World as needed and performs a ``digest'' step, in which it interprets each predicted state and extracts task-relevant evidence for selecting the best action. Experiments show that MKD2World-9B achieves an EleF1 score of 26.50 on the out-of-domain test set, outperforming GPT-5.6-Sol(14.98) by 11.52 points. Across Online-Mind2Web tasks, MKD2World-9B improves the success rate of the Qwen3.5-35B web agent from 22.94% to 37.61%, achieving competitive performance among leading open-source GUI world models. Code is available at https://github.com/OmniAI-Lab-CUHKSZ/MKD2World.

</details>


### [99] [LungMend: A Healthy-Only Latent Diffusion Shape Prior for Lung Mask Correction in Severe Tuberculosis CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06556)
*Rezkellah Noureddine Khiat,Pierre-Yves Brillet,Catalin Fetita*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LungMend 是一种后处理模型，利用仅基于健康解剖先验训练的潜在扩散模型，修复 R231 等肺分割模型在重度结核 CT 上漏检空洞与实变的错误掩膜。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有肺分割模型（如 R231）在重度结核 CT 上失效：空洞和实变在影像上类似非肺组织，被排除在肺掩膜之外，导致分割不完整。

Method: 提出 LungMend 后处理模型。训练仅使用健康肺解剖：在健康肺数据上，对掩膜施加人工破坏，训练潜在扩散模型从“错误掩膜 + 骨骼解剖”中恢复完整肺掩膜；整个过程不使用任何病理数据，也不依赖 CT 强度信息。

Result: LungMend 能够补回 R231 漏掉的空洞与实变区域，并在专家标注切片上提升分割精度。

Conclusion: 以健康解剖为唯一先验的后处理范式，可在无病理监督的条件下有效修正病灶性分割缺失，为重度病变场景下的肺分割提供可行补充方案。

Abstract: Lung segmentation models such as R231 are widely used, but they fail on severe tuberculosis (TB) CT: cavities and consolidations look like non-lung tissue and are left out of the mask. We present LungMend, a post-processing model that corrects such masks using only healthy anatomy. A latent diffusion model, trained on healthy lungs with synthetically corrupted masks, learns to recover the complete lung from the faulty mask and the bone anatomy, without any pathological data or CT intensities. LungMend recovers cavities and consolidations that R231 misses and improves accuracy on expert-annotated slices. Code: https://github.com/noureddinekhiati/LungMend

</details>


### [100] [BudgetSplat: Budget-Controllable Generalizable Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06593)
*Yuxi Hu,Jun Zhang,Wei Mao,Yuchen Rao,Friedrich Fraundorfer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出BudgetSplat，一个预算可控的前馈3D高斯泼溅框架，可按用户指定的保留比例自适应压缩高斯原语，在保持渲染质量的同时显著降低存储与渲染开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有像素对齐的前馈高斯方法生成大量冗余高斯原语，导致表示大小与渲染成本随图像分辨率和输入视角数快速增长；且压缩通常被简化为重要性排序，忽略空间覆盖与跨视角冗余。

Method: 将压缩建模为重要性估计、空间覆盖与跨视角冗余的联合问题；设计Intra-Cross Mask Supervision（结构、覆盖、冗余感知的重要性监督），预算条件特征调制（按保留比例调节不透明度与属性），以及Coverage-Aware Budget Selection（patch级预选、视角级不透明度排序、3D冗余抑制）。单一模型支持多视角数与多测试预算，无需逐预算重训或逐场景压缩。

Result: 在室内重建与跨数据集泛化实验中，不同输入视角数和保留比例下均呈现一致的质量—大小权衡，显著减少表示存储与渲染成本，同时保持渲染质量。

Conclusion: 将高斯压缩表述为联合优化问题并配合预算条件调制，可在单一前馈模型中实现可控、可泛化的紧凑高斯表示，为高效可泛化的新视角合成提供实用方案。

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D Gaussian Splatting enables efficient generalizable novel view synthesis, but existing pixel-aligned methods often generate highly redundant Gaussian primitives, causing the final representation size and rendering cost to grow rapidly with image resolution and the number of input views. We propose BudgetSplat, a budget-controllable feed-forward framework that adapts the final Gaussian representation to a user-specified preservation ratio. Rather than treating compaction as simple importance ranking, we formulate it as a joint problem of importance estimation, spatial coverage, and cross-view redundancy. A single trained model supports varying numbers of context views and multiple test-time budgets without per-budget retraining or per-scene compression. To realize this formulation, Intra-Cross Mask Supervision provides structure-, coverage-, and redundancy-aware guidance for learning Gaussian importance, while budget-conditioned feature modulation adapts Gaussian opacity and attributes to the requested preservation ratio. Coverage-Aware Budget Selection further combines patch-wise pre-selection, view-wise opacity ranking, and 3D redundancy suppression to construct compact yet spatially distributed Gaussian representations. Experiments on indoor reconstruction and cross-dataset generalization demonstrate consistent quality--size trade-offs across different input view counts and preservation ratios, substantially reducing final representation storage and rendering cost while preserving rendering quality.

</details>


### [101] [SpikePose: Lightweight Video Human Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06594)
*Yunhao Huang,Kaushik Bhargav Sivangi,Tianyi Yu,Fani Deligianni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SpikePose：轻量级视频人体姿态估计器，采用帧重置单步脉冲编码器 + 运动对齐热图记忆（MAM）。相较架构匹配的ANN，算术能耗降低91.4%，PCK_HB(0.5)仅下降1.12点；引入MAM使速度/加速度误差分别降低28.13%/31.46%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高效视频人体姿态估计需在定位精度、运动质量与能耗之间取得平衡；脉冲神经网络（SNN）在事件驱动任务上有能效优势，但在大规模非事件驱动视频任务上与经典ANN存在性能差距，本文旨在缩小该差距。

Method: 核心为帧重置（frame-reset）、单步（single-step）的脉冲编码器，逐帧提供空间证据；外接轻量运动对齐热图记忆模块（MAM），估计关节位移、对齐历史热图并与当前预测自适应融合。时间推理被限制在脉冲骨干之外的紧凑轻量模块中。评估指标包括空间精度、运动误差与估算算术能耗，数据集为MPII与固定子集的NTU60 Cross-Subject。

Result: 与架构匹配的含MAM的ANN相比，SpikePose算术能耗降低91.4%，代价是头骨归一化PCK@0.5（PCK_HB(0.5)）仅下降1.12点。在SpikePose内部，加入MAM使速度误差降低28.13%、加速度误差降低31.46%，定位略有提升，仅增加0.003M参数与每帧0.0023解析GFLOPs。

Conclusion: 将时间推理外置于脉冲骨干的轻量设计，可在极低能耗代价下保持接近ANN的定位精度并显著改善运动质量，为SNN在非事件驱动大规模视频任务上的应用提供了可行路径；代码将在接收后于GitHub发布。

Abstract: Efficient video human pose estimation must balance localisation accuracy, motion quality, and energy cost. We present SpikePose, a lightweight video pose estimator that pairs a frame-reset, single-step spiking encoder with motion-aligned heatmap memory (MAM). The encoder supplies per-frame spatial evidence, while MAM estimates joint displacement, aligns historical heatmaps, and adaptively fuses them with current predictions. By confining temporal reasoning to a compact, lightweight module outside the spiking backbone, SpikePose helps bridge the gap between spiking and classical Artificial Neural Networks (ANN) on large-scale, non-event-driven video tasks. We evaluate spatial accuracy, motion errors, and estimated arithmetic energy on MPII and a fixed NTU60 Cross-Subject subset. Against the architecture-matched ANN with MAM, SpikePose cuts estimated arithmetic energy by 91.4% at a cost of just 1.12 points in head-bone-normalised PCK at 0.5 threshold (PCK_HB(0.5)). Within SpikePose, adding MAM reduces velocity and acceleration errors by 28.13% and 31.46% while slightly improving localisation, at a cost of only 0.003M parameters and 0.0023 analytical GFLOPs per frame. Code will be released on GitHub upon acceptance.

</details>


### [102] [Learning to Track Cells from Sparse Annotations in 3D Time-Lapse Microscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06624)
*Yusuke Uchida*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出集成稀疏标注检测、候选连接校准与谱系图事件联合选择的细胞追踪流程，在两斑马鱼胚胎199个视频的5折交叉验证中取得0.9762总分，联合选择较顺序方法提升0.0105，主要来自分裂恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D延时显微成像中重建细胞谱系需区分细胞运动与分裂并推断时序一致的连接；但仅部分细胞被标注时，将未标注区域视为背景或错误对应关系的训练方式会产生问题。

Method: 流程含三部分：(1)检测：融合2.5D与3D模型输出，抑制未标注细胞处的错误背景监督；(2)校准：利用稠密光流的运动特征、学习型细胞匹配模型的对应特征，以及识别分裂前细胞、分裂母细胞、分裂后子细胞模型的特征，校准普通连接与分裂概率；(3)联合选择：以由评价指标导出的代理目标，联合选择普通连接、以子细胞对表示的分裂和节点纳入，随后进行间隙填补与时间坐标精化。

Result: 在2个斑马鱼胚胎的199个视频上5折交叉验证总体得分为0.9762；在候选与校准值固定时，联合选择比允许替换已有分裂并重优化普通连接的顺序方法高0.0105，提升主要源于分裂恢复。

Conclusion: 以评价指标为导向的联合选择优于顺序式后处理，稀疏标注下鲁棒的检测与特征校准可有效用于3D细胞谱系重建。

Abstract: Reconstructing cell lineages from 3D time-lapse microscopy requires distinguishing cell motion from division and inferring temporally consistent connections. When only a subset of cells is annotated, however, training procedures that treat unannotated regions as background or incorrect correspondences can be problematic. We propose a cell tracking pipeline that integrates detection from sparse annotations, calibration of candidate connections using image features, and joint selection of lineage graph events. For detection, we combine the outputs of 2.5D and 3D models trained to suppress erroneous background supervision at unannotated cells. We then calibrate ordinary link and division probabilities using motion features from dense flow, correspondence features from a learned cell-matching model, and features from models that identify cells immediately before division, dividing parents, and daughters immediately after division.
